Return `t/10`, a `.`, then `t%10` (for example `95000` ms gives `1.6`, `0` gives `0.0`).\n- `Cost(usd float64) string`: return `fmt.Sprintf(\"%.4f\", usd)`, exactly four decimal places (zero gives `0.0000`).\n- Both functions are pure, take no filters and read no input.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC format-minutes-rounding ===\nIntent: Minutes applies the (ms + 3000) / 6000 tenths rule across the rounding boundaries.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/format\", \"TestMinutesRounding\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC format-minutes-rounding ===\n\n=== AC format-minutes-zero ===\nIntent: Minutes of zero milliseconds is written as tenths with one decimal digit.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/format\", \"TestMinutesZero\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC format-minutes-zero ===\n\n=== AC format-cost-four-places ===\nIntent: Cost always writes exactly four decimal places.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/format\", \"TestCostFourPlaces\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC format-cost-four-places ===\n\n=== AC format-cost-zero ===\nIntent: Cost of zero is written with four decimal places.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/format\", \"TestCostZero\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC format-cost-zero ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Standard library only; no third-party module.\n- Formatters never read a log, a filter, or a report answer; every value comes from the argument.\n- Test expectations are derived from the formula, not typed as hand-calculated answers.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-ReportSummary.md ===\n# FEATURE: Report Summary\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | The summary report: one CSV row of totals over the filtered records plus the whole-file skipped count. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-CliExit.md, FEATURE-ValueFormat.md |\n| Provides    | ddkstat summary |\n| Consumes    | cli.Register, format.Minutes, format.Cost, logread.Record |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ddkstat summary [options] <log>` answers how many calls ran, how many were skipped, and what they used and cost.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Header: `records,skipped,succeeded,failed,first,last,input_tokens,cached_tokens,output_tokens,cost_usd,minutes`.\n- One data row over the filtered records, `\\n` line endings.\n- `records` is the filtered record count; `succeeded` and `failed` split it by the failed rule; token columns are sums; `cost_usd` uses `format.Cost`; `minutes` uses `format.Minutes` of the summed `elapsed_ms`.\n- `skipped` is the whole-file skipped-line count and ignores every filter.\n- `first` and `last` are the earliest and latest start dates of the filtered records, empty when none match.\n- Aggregation (`SummaryAggregate`) and writing (`WriteSummary(w io.Writer, records []logread.Record, skipped int) error`) are separate functions in `internal/report`; `internal/cli` registers the writer as `summary` with `cli.Register`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC summary-aggregate ===\nIntent: SummaryAggregate sums counts, tokens, cost and duration over records built by the test.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/report\", \"TestSummaryAggregate\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC summary-aggregate ===\n\n=== AC summary-empty-first-last ===\nIntent: With no matching record, first and last are empty and counters are zero.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/report\", \"TestSummaryEmptyFirstLast\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC summary-empty-first-last ===\n\n=== AC summary-skipped-ignores-filters ===\nIntent: The skipped count stays the whole-file count regardless of target and date filters.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/report\", \"TestSummarySkippedIgnoresFilters\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC summary-skipped-ignores-filters ===\n\n=== AC summary-write ===\nIntent: WriteSummary writes the declared header and one row with the declared column order.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/report\", \"TestSummaryWrite\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC summary-write ===\n\n=== AC summary-run-via-cli ===\nIntent: cli.Run dispatches the summary report over a test-written log and exits zero with a parseable CSV.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/cli\", \"TestRunSummaryReport\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC summary-run-via-cli ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Standard library only; no database; the log is never written.\n- No report value is hardcoded; every number comes from the log being read.\n- Skipped-line warnings go to standard error, never into the CSV.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-ReportCommands.md ===\n# FEATURE: Report Commands\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | The commands report: one CSV row per Drydock command with a TOTAL row. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-CliExit.md, FEATURE-ValueFormat.md |\n| Provides    | ddkstat commands |\n| Consumes    | cli.Register, format.Minutes, logread.Record |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ddkstat commands [options] <log>` shows how often each Drydock command called an LLM and what it consumed.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Header: `command,calls,failed,input_tokens,cached_tokens,output_tokens,minutes`.\n- One row per `job.command_name`, ordered by calls descending, then command ascending by byte value.\n- Final row `TOTAL,...` over all filtered records.\n- Minutes use `format.Minutes` of the group's summed `elapsed_ms`.\n- When no record matches, the output is the header and `TOTAL,0,0,0,0,0,0.0`.\n- Text values are written unquoted and unescaped.\n- `internal/report` holds the aggregate and write functions; `internal/cli` registers the writer as `commands`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC commands-ordering ===\nIntent: Rows are ordered by calls descending then command ascending, including ties.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/report\", \"TestCommandsOrdering\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC commands-ordering ===\n\n=== AC commands-total ===\nIntent: The TOTAL row sums calls, failures, tokens and minutes over all filtered records.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/report\", \"TestCommandsTotal\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC commands-total ===\n\n=== AC commands-empty ===\nIntent: With no matching record the report is the header plus a zero TOTAL row.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/report\", \"TestCommandsEmpty\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC commands-empty ===\n\n=== AC commands-write ===\nIntent: The writer emits the declared header and columns.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/report\", \"TestCommandsWrite\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC commands-write ===\n\n=== AC commands-run-via-cli ===\nIntent: cli.Run dispatches the commands report over a test-written log, exits zero and writes parseable CSV.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/cli\", \"TestRunCommandsReport\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC commands-run-via-cli ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Standard library only; no database; the log is never written.\n- No row, count or total is hardcoded; all values come from the log being read.\n- Text values are never quoted or escaped.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-ReportTargets.md ===\n# FEATURE: Report Targets\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-ValueFormat.md ===\n# FEATURE: Value Format\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | Shared text formatters for minutes (tenths of a minute) and cost (four decimal places). |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides    | format.Minutes, format.Cost |\n| Consumes    | go.mod |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nEvery report that prints a minutes column or a cost column uses these two pure functions in `internal/format`, so the rounding rule lives in exactly one place.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `Minutes(ms int64) string`: let `t = (ms + 3000) / 6000` using integer division. Return `t/10`, a `.`, then `t%10` (for example `95000` ms gives `1.6`, `0` gives `0.0`).\n- `Cost(usd float64) string`: return `fmt.Sprintf(\"%.4f\", usd)`, exactly four decimal places (zero gives `0.0000`).\n- Both functions are pure, take no filters and read no input.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC format-minutes-rounding ===\nIntent: Minutes applies the (ms + 3000) / 6000 tenths rule across the rounding boundaries.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/format\", \"TestMinutesRounding\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC format-minutes-rounding ===\n\n=== AC format-minutes-zero ===\nIntent: Minutes of zero milliseconds is written as tenths with one decimal digit.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/format\", \"TestMinutesZero\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC format-minutes-zero ===\n\n=== AC format-cost-four-places ===\nIntent: Cost always writes exactly four decimal places.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/format\", \"TestCostFourPlaces\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC format-cost-four-places ===\n\n=== AC format-cost-zero ===\nIntent: Cost of zero is written with four decimal places.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/format\", \"TestCostZero\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC format-cost-zero argument.\n- Test expectations are derived from the formula, not typed as hand-calculated answers.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-ReportSummary.md ===\n# FEATURE: Report Summary\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261026 V1 |\n| Description | The summary report: one CSV row of totals over the filtered records plus the whole-file skipped count. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-CliExit.md, FEATURE-ValueFormat.md |\n| Provides    | ddkstat summary |\n| Consumes    | cli.Register, format.Minutes, format.Cost, logread.Record |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ddkstat summary [options] <log>` answers how many calls ran, how many were skipped, and what they used and cost.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Header: `records,skipped,succeeded,failed,first,last,input_tokens,cached_tokens,output_tokens,cost_usd,minutes`.\n- One data row over the filtered records, `\\n` line endings.\n- `records` is the filtered record count; `succeeded` and `failed` split it by the failed rule; token columns are sums; `cost_usd` uses `format.Cost`; `minutes` uses `format.Minutes` of the summed `elapsed_ms`.\n- `skipped` is the whole-file skipped-line count and ignores every filter.\n- `first` and `last` are the earliest and latest start dates of the filtered records, empty when none match.\n- Aggregation (`SummaryAggregate`) and writing (`WriteSummary(w io.Writer, records []logread.Record, skipped int) error`) are separate functions in `internal/report`; `internal/cli` registers the writer as `summary` with `cli.Register`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC summary-aggregate ===\nIntent: SummaryAggregate sums counts, tokens, cost and duration over records built by the test.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/report\", \"TestSummaryAggregate\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC summary-aggregate ===\n\n=== AC summary-empty-first-last ===\nIntent: With no matching record, first and last are empty and counters are zero.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/report\", \"TestSummaryEmptyFirstLast\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC summary-empty-first-last ===\n\n=== AC summary-skipped-ignores-filters ===\nIntent: The skipped count stays the whole-file count regardless of target and date filters.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/report\", \"TestSummarySkippedIgnoresFilters\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nprint(result.stderr)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC summary-skipped-ignores-filters ===\n\n=== AC summary-write ===\nIntent: WriteSummary writes the declared header and one row with the declared result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC summary-run-via-cli ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Standard library only; no database; the log is never written.\n- No report
